<TEXT>
<html>
<head>
<title></title>

<body style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif" bgcolor="#ffffff" text="#000000">
<div>
<hr style="BORDER-TOP: black 4px solid; HEIGHT: 10px; BORDER-RIGHT: medium none; BORDER-BOTTOM: black 1px solid; COLOR: #ffffff; TEXT-ALIGN: center; MARGIN-LEFT: auto; BORDER-LEFT: medium none; BACKGROUND-COLOR: #ffffff; MARGIN-RIGHT: auto" align="center">
<br>
<div id="DSPFPageHeader"></div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: right; FONT: bold 10pt/1.25 'Times New Roman', Times, serif"><u>Exhibit 99.1</u></div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; TEXT-ALIGN: center; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">&#160;</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; TEXT-ALIGN: center; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">Kamada Reports Financial Results for the Fourth Quarter and Full-Year 2016</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-STYLE: italic; TEXT-ALIGN: center; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">Total revenues for 2016 were $77.5 million, an 11% increase over 2015</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-STYLE: italic; TEXT-ALIGN: center; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">Full year 2016 Proprietary Product revenues up over 30%</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-STYLE: italic; TEXT-ALIGN: center; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">Reaffirms revenue guidance of $100 million for 2017</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-STYLE: italic; TEXT-ALIGN: center; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">Conference Call Today at 8:30am Eastern Time</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><font style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">NESS ZIONA, Israel -- February 6, 2017 --</font> Kamada Ltd. (Nasdaq: KMDA) (KMDA.TA), a plasma-derived protein therapeutics company focused on orphan indications, announced today financial results for the three and twelve months ended December 31, 2016.</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">&#8220;We are very pleased with our accomplishments in 2016,&#8221; said Amir London, Chief Executive Officer.&#160; &#8220;We met our revenue guidance for full year 2016, with over 30% growth in our Propriety Products revenues compared with the previous year, and look forward to a higher rate of revenue growth in this segment in 2017.&#160; Moreover, the extension of our supply agreement with Shire for GLASSIA<font style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Symbol, serif">&#210;</font> through 2020 underscores <font style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif">Shire&#8217;s solid outlook for higher long-term demand for </font>GLASSIA<font style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Symbol, serif">&#210;</font><font style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif"> in the U.S. </font>This minimum revenue commitment by Shire - $237 million over four years - further strengthens our confidence in achieving our guidance of $100 million in total revenues in 2017 and represents further growth in the following years.&#8221;</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">&#8220;Additionally, we achieved a number of key development milestones during 2016, and look forward to multiple additional regulatory and clinical development-related value-creating milestones in 2017. We were pleased to announce last August that our U.S. Phase 2 study of inhaled Alpha-1 Antitrypsin (AAT) for the treatment of AAT Deficiency met its primary endpoint of a significant increase in endothelial lining fluid inhibitory capacity.&#160; We recently submitted this data to the European Medicines Agency (EMA) in support of our filed Inhaled AAT Marketing Authorization Application (MAA) as part of our response to the day 120 comments, and will also use it in our discussions with the FDA in order to identify a regulatory path for inhaled AAT in the U.S.,&#8221; added Mr. London.</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">Financial Highlights for the Twelve Months Ended December 31, 2016:</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
<table id="z05dab127e1604e229420d7d730715333" class="DSPFListTable" style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; WIDTH: 100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0">
<tr>
<td style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Symbol, serif; VERTICAL-ALIGN: top; WIDTH: 18pt; align: right">&#183;</td>
<td style="VERTICAL-ALIGN: top; TEXT-ALIGN: left; WIDTH: auto">
<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif">Total revenues for 2016 were $77.5 million, an 11% increase from $69.9 million in 2015.</div>
</td>
</tr>
</table>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
<table id="z9de486d60dc040a8be7f5f8cbac70323" class="DSPFListTable" style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; WIDTH: 100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0">
<tr>
<td style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Symbol, serif; VERTICAL-ALIGN: top; WIDTH: 18pt; align: right">&#183;</td>
<td style="VERTICAL-ALIGN: top; TEXT-ALIGN: left; WIDTH: auto">
<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif">Revenues from the Proprietary Products segment in 2016 were $56.0 million, a 30.3% increase from $43.0 million in 2015.</div>
</td>
</tr>
</table>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
<table id="zd796589265ed4159accae2f6ccfcac11" class="DSPFListTable" style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; WIDTH: 100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0">

Minimum revenue for Kamada for the four-year period from 2017 to 2020 will reach approximately $237 million and may be expanded to $288 million during that period. This extension represents the fourth time the companies have extended the contract for manufacturing supply of GLASSIA&#174; since the start of the strategic relationship in 2010.</div>
</td>
</tr>
</table>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
<table id="z49930384c5eb41a7b560be4191e2c665" class="DSPFListTable" style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; WIDTH: 100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0">

<td style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Symbol, serif; VERTICAL-ALIGN: top; WIDTH: 18pt; align: right">&#183;</td>
<td style="VERTICAL-ALIGN: top; TEXT-ALIGN: left; WIDTH: auto">
<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif">Announced plans for a Phase 2/3 clinical trial of Alpha-1 Antitrypsin IV (G1-AAT IV) for the treatment of Graft-Versus-Host Disease (GvHD) in collaboration with Shire plc.&#160; This U.S. clinical trial will be a two-part, multi-center, prospective study to evaluate the safety and efficacy of G1-AAT IV as an add-on biopharmacotherapy to conventional steroid treatment in up to 168 patients with acute GvHD (aGvHD) with lower gastrointestinal involvement. G1-AAT IV previously received orphan drug designation from the FDA and EMA for the treatment of GvHD, and an Investigational New Drug Application was submitted to the FDA earlier this year.</div>
</td>
</tr>
</table>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">&#8220;We are pleased with 2016&#8217;s strong financial performance, including meeting our revenue guidance, reducing our reported net losses and generating a positive cash flow in the fourth quarter and in the full year of 2016,&#8221; said Gil Efron, Deputy CEO and Chief Financial Officer.&#160; &#8220;Growth of 30% in revenues from our Proprietary Products drove our strong financial performance in 2016, resulting in a net loss of $6.7 million, a 40% year-over-year decrease.&#8221;</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">&#8220;As a consequence of our expected growth in total revenues in 2017 to $100 million, a projected increase of nearly 30% year-over-year, we project that Kamada will be profitable in 2017, even while continuing our R&amp;D investment in support of our product pipeline,&#8221; added Mr. Efron.</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">Full Year 2016 versus 2015</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">Total revenues for 2016 were $77.5 million, up 11% as compared to $69.9 million for 2015. Revenues from the Proprietary Products segment for 2016 were $56.0 million, up 30% as compared to $43.0 million in 2015.&#160; Distributed Products revenue was $21.5 million for 2016, a decrease of 20% compared to $27.0 million <font style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif">in </font>2015.</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">Gross profit for 2016 grew 40% to $21.7 million, compared to $15.8 million during 2015.&#160; <font style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif">Gross margin increased to 27.9% for 2016 from 22.6% in 2015.</font></div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">R&amp;D expenses in 2016 were $16.2 million, a slight decrease compared to $16.5 million in 2015.&#160; Selling, general and administrative expenses of 2016 were $10.9 million, an increase of 2% compared to $10.7 million in 2015.&#160; For 2016, the Company reported an operating loss of $5.5 million, compared with an operating loss of $11.4 million in 2015.&#160; The net loss for 2016 was $6.7 million, or ($0.18) per diluted share, compared with a net loss of $11.3 million, or ($0.31) per diluted share, in <font style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif">the same period of</font> 2015.</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25; BACKGROUND-COLOR: #ffffff">Negative Adjusted EBITDA for 2016 was $0.9 million, compared with negative Adjusted EBITDA of $6.3 million for 2015.&#160; Adjusted net loss was $5.6 million in 2016, compared with an adjusted net loss of $9.4 million in 2015.</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">Fourth Quarter 2016 Financial Results Compared to Fourth Quarter 2015 Financial Results</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">Total revenues for the fourth quarter of 2016 of $24.3 million decreased by 5.5% as compared to $25.6 million in the fourth quarter of 2015. Revenues from the Proprietary Products segment were $17.7 million<font style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif">&#160;</font>for the fourth quarter of 2016,<font style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif">&#160;</font>in-line with the fourth quarter of 2015.&#160; Distributed Products revenue was $6.6 million, a decrease of 19.3% as compared with $8.1 million in the fourth quarter 2015.</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">Gross profit for the fourth quarter of 2016 in the Proprietary Products segment was $4.1 million, a decrease of 40.3% compared with $6.9 million in the fourth quarter of 2015, principally due to an unexpected shutdown of our manufacturing plant and an inventory write-off for a total amount of $2.6 million.&#160; <font style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif">Gross margin was 23.2%, a decline from 39.9% in the same period of 2015.</font></div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div id="DSPFPageBreakArea" style="MARGIN-BOTTOM: 10pt; CLEAR: both; MARGIN-TOP: 10pt">
<div id="DSPFPageBreak" style="PAGE-BREAK-AFTER: always">
<hr style="BORDER-LEFT-WIDTH: 0px; HEIGHT: 2px; BORDER-RIGHT-WIDTH: 0px; BORDER-BOTTOM-WIDTH: 0px; COLOR: #000000; CLEAR: both; MARGIN: 4px 0px; BORDER-TOP-WIDTH: 0px; WIDTH: 100%; BACKGROUND-COLOR: #000000" noshade="noshade">
</div>
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">R&amp;D expenses in the fourth quarter of 2016 were $4.2 million, in-line with the $4.4 million recorded in the same period of 2015.&#160; Selling, general and administrative expenses were $2.6 million, down 7% from the $2.8 million in the same period in 2015.&#160; Operating loss in the fourth quarter of 2016 was ($1.9) million, as compared to operating income of $0.8 million recorded in the same period of 2015.&#160; Net loss for the fourth quarter of 2016 was ($1.8) million, or ($0.05) per diluted share, compared to net income of $1.0 million, or $0.03 per diluted share, in the same period of 2015.</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25; BACKGROUND-COLOR: #ffffff">Negative Adjusted EBITDA for the fourth quarter of 2016 was ($1.0) million, compared with Positive Adjusted EBITDA for the fourth quarter of 2015 of $2.0 million.&#160; Adjusted net loss for the fourth quarter of 2016 was ($1.8) million, compared with adjusted net income of $1.4 million in the fourth quarter of 2015.</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">&#160;</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">Balance Sheet Highlights</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25; BACKGROUND-COLOR: #ffffff">As of December 31, 2016, Kamada had cash, cash equivalents and short-term investments of $28.6 million, compared with $28.3 million as of December 31, 2015. During 2016, the Company generated $1.9 million in cash from operation operations and used $2.6 million for capital expenditures.&#160;</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">&#160;</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">2017 Revenue Guidance</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: left; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">For the year ending December 31, 2017, Kamada expects total revenues to be&#160; $100 million.</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-STYLE: italic; TEXT-ALIGN: justify; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">About Kamada</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; TEXT-ALIGN: justify; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">Kamada Ltd. is focused on plasma-derived protein therapeutics for orphan indications, and has a commercial product portfolio and a robust late-stage product pipeline. The Company uses its proprietary platform technology and know-how for the extraction and purification of proteins from human plasma to produce Alpha-1 Antitrypsin (AAT) in a highly-purified, liquid form, as well as other plasma-derived Immune globulins.&#160; AAT is a protein derived from human plasma with known and newly-discovered therapeutic roles given its immunomodulatory, anti-inflammatory, tissue-protective and antimicrobial properties. The Company&#8217;s flagship product is GLASSIA&#174;, the first and only liquid, ready-to-use, intravenous plasma-derived AAT product approved by the U.S. Food and Drug Administration. Kamada markets GLASSIA&#174; in the U.S. through a strategic partnership with Baxalta (now part of Shire plc) and in other counties through local distributors.&#160; In addition to GLASSIA&#174;, Kamada has a product line of seven other pharmaceutical products administered by injection or infusion, that are marketed through distributors in more than 15 countries, including Israel, Russia, Brazil, India and other countries in Latin America and Asia. Kamada has five late-stage plasma-derived protein products in development, including an inhaled formulation of AAT for the treatment of AAT deficiency for which a MAA was submitted to the EMA after completing a pivotal Phase 2/3 clinical trials in Europe. &#160;Kamada has also completed its Phase 2 clinical trials in the U.S for the treatment of AAT deficiency with inhaled AAT. In addition, Kamada's intravenous AAT is in development for other indications such as type-1 diabetes, GvHD and prevention of lung transplant rejection. Kamada also leverages its expertise and presence in the plasma-derived protein therapeutics market by distributing more than 10 complementary products in Israel that are manufactured by third parties.</div>

<div style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25"><br style="LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">
</div>

<div style="FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-STYLE: italic; TEXT-ALIGN: justify; LINE-HEIGHT: 1.25">Cautionary Note Regarding Forward-Looking Statements</div>
